- **commit-pr-guardian**: Use this agent for repository hygiene, Conventional Commits enforcement, and PR management within the Syncwright ecosystem. This agent specializes in generating proper commit messages with domain-specific scopes, validating PR quality, and maintaining Git workflow standards. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: Developer needs to commit conflict resolution improvements. user: 'I improved the AI confidence scoring algorithm' assistant: 'I'll use the commit-pr-guardian agent to generate a proper commit message following Conventional Commits with the appropriate scope.' &lt;commentary&gt;Since this involves co… ([NeuBlink/syncwright](https://github.com/NeuBlink/syncwright/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/commit-pr-guardian.md) · ★2)

- **committee-design-usability-tester**: Usability tester, a worker in the committee's design chapter. - **committee-design-ux-designer**: UX designer, a member of the committee's design chapter.
